```
def full_conv(a):
    n=a.size; size=2*n-1; L=1 << (size-1).bit_length(); fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L); b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,L); return b[:size]
```
Small n overhead but okay. Could use np.convolve for n<=256. For coordinate line search, we need base from full conv.

`score_M(a)` returns M=max conv (score=2nM/(sum?) since normalized). Actually if a normalized sum=1, `max(full_conv(a))`. Score=2*n*M. We'll store M. If a not normalized, compute `max(b)/(sum)^2` maybe.

`normalize(a)`: a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); if s<1e-15 return zeros; return a/s.

`make_positive(a, eps)`: enforce small positive for LP? Could use a = (1-eps)*a + eps/n with eps=1e-6.

```
def coord_descent(a, max_time, rng, verbose=False):
    a = normalize(a.copy())
    n=a.size
    b=full_conv(a); M=b.max(); cur_score=2*n*M
    best=a.copy(); best_score=cur_score; best_M=M
    t0=time.monotonic()
    cycles=0
    stall=0
    # candidate multipliers relative to current x: include 0 and geometric
    mults = [0.0, 0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.25, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0, 5.0, 8.0]
    # But if x=0 need absolute small/guess: add np.array([1/n*0.1, 0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2])
    while time...:
       order=rng.permutation(n)
       improved=False
       for j in order:
          x=a[j]
          # line search via grid+local
          y_best, y_score = find_best_y(a,b,j,x,?)
          if y_score * n? compare to cur M? Actually score_y internal = M_y / S^2. Since normalized current sum=1, if update unnormalized sum S, M_y/S² = new normalized M. We want minimize new M. cur_M = b.max(). So if y_score (which is normalized M) < M * (1 - 1e-10), update.
          if update:
             a[j]=y_best; s=a.sum(); if s>0: a/=s? Wait if we changed unnormalized, the normalized sequence is a/s. For coordinate descent, the candidate normalized after updating j and zeroing? If we set a[j]=y, others x, sum S, normalized new = a/S. Its M = M_y/S². We can either store unnormalized a and compute score via M_y/S², then after update set a = a/S to keep sum1. But if we only track b in normalized terms, after setting a=S? Better: maintain a normalized. Candidate normalized equivalent to a_norm_candidate = (a with j=y)/S. We can update a[j]=y/S? Wait others unchanged in unnormalized become (1/S)*old. So not just changing one normalized coordinate. Implement: old normalized a. To evaluate y, S=1 - x + y. For coordinate j current x. Unchanged sum S0. Candidate y: delta? b_y = b with affected line updated. But b currently includes old x; if we set a[j]=y, we can update affected lags as earlier with x old. S_y = S0 - x + y. Norm_M_y = max(max_unaffected, affected_y)/(S_y*S_y). We can evaluate. If best_y improves norm_M, update a[j]=y, b=updated b (for line), S=a.sum() (or S_y). No need to normalize until drift. Periodically a/=S; b/=S²; S=1.
```

```
   # For each k in active: c = sum a[i]*a[k-i]
```

Jacobian:
```
def slsqp_ineq_jac(x, active, n):
  a=x[:n]
  m=active.size; J=np.empty((m,n+1))
  for idx,k in active:
     # d c_k / d a_j = 2 a[k-j] if 0≤k-j<n else 0
     j_vals = k - np.arange(n)
     valid = (j_vals>=0)&(j_vals<n)
     pows = 2*a[j_vals[valid]]
     J[idx, :n] = 0; J[idx, valid] = -pows
     J[idx,-1]=1
  return J
```
Equality: sum a =1. `slsqp_eq(x,n): sum x[:n]-1`, jac row ones on a, 0 t.

```
def coord_descent(a, time_limit, rng, initial_best=None):
    a=normalize(a.copy())
    S=1.0
    b=full_conv(a)
    cur_M = b.max()
    best_a=a.copy(); best_M=cur_M; best_score? we can return M.
    t0=time.monotonic()
    cycles=0
    simple_tol=1e-12
    # precompute? maybe candidate multipliers arrays top-level
    while time...
       order = rng.permutation(n)
       improved=False
       for j in order:
          # maybe if cur_M - best_M > small? current a may be worse than best due to accepted worse? We only accept improvements, so cur_M=best_M? Actually after coordinate descent all updates improve cur_M, so cur_M=best_M. But if simulate anneal accept worse, then not. We can keep best separate. If current worse, line search may recover.
          y_best, aff_best, new_ratio = coordinate_step(a,b,S,j, candidates maybe return)
          if new_ratio < cur_M - 1e-14:
             # update
             b[j:j+n]=aff_best
             a[j]=y_best
             S = S - x + y_best
             cur_M = new_ratio
             if cur_M < best_M - 1e-15:
                best_M=cur_M; best_a=a.copy()
             improved=True
             if S > 2 or S < 0.5:
                a/=S; b/=S*S; S=1.0
                cur_M=b.max() # should equal best_M
       cycles +=1
       if not improved or cycles%? ...:
          # stall: random perturbation around best
          a = normalize(best_a + sigma*rng.standard_normal(n))
          # To avoid zeros, maybe add base: if min zero, okay.
          b=full_conv(a); S=1; cur_M=b.max()
          if cur_M < best_M: update
          # maybe if too many stalls break
          if stall_count>...: break?
          sigma maybe 0.02, 0.05
```

Coordinate_step:
```
def coord_step(a,b,S,j,cur_ratio,masks, specific maybe):
   n=a.size
   x=a[j]
   block = b[j:j+n]
   unaff_mask = masks[j]
   if unaff_mask.any(): unaff_max = np.max(b[unaff_mask]) else -1e-300
   # self position relative j (0/n?) ind starts at j, k=2j -> relative j. if 0<=j<n yes.
   self_mask = np.zeros(n, bool); self_mask[j]=True
   # candidate y array
   amp = max(S/n, 0.25*np.sqrt(max(cur_ratio,0.0)), 1e-8)  # cur_ratio is M_norm maybe
   cands = np.concatenate((np.array([0.0,x]), x*COORD_MULTS, amp*COORD_ABS_FACTORS, S*COORD_SUM_FACTORS))
   cands = cands[np.isfinite(cands)]
   cands = np.clip(cands, 0.0, max(S, x*2?))  # cannot exceed S? if y can exceed S? Nonnegative sum S; if y > S, sum=S-x+y >y, possible? But coordinate can be > old sum, new sum larger. In unnormalized, y can exceed S; normalized coordinate can be <1 eventually. Allow y_max = max(S, x*2, S + 0.5*amp)? Because if x=0, all mass elsewhere, adding y huge while others unchanged: new sum ~ y, ratio at y→∞ tends? Could coordinate optimum be y>>S? If others sum S, setting y >> S gives normalized a_j→1, M≈1, ratio≈1. If current cur_ratio maybe <1, not helpful. So optimum likely y ≤ some multiple of S. Cap y_max = max(S, 4*amp) to be safe.
   y_max = max(S, 4*amp)
   cands = cands[cands <= y_max]
   # ensure x included
   ...
   # initial eval
   delta = cands[:,None] - x
   aff = block[None,:] + 2*delta*a[None,:] + self_mask[None,:]*delta*delta
   M_abs = np.maximum(aff.max(axis=1), unaff_max)
   Sy = S - x + cands
   ratios = M_abs/(Sy*Sy)
   # If any Sy<=0 or M_abs<0 set inf
   best_idx = argmin ratios
   y0=cands[best_idx]; ratio0=ratios[best_idx]
   # refine if interval positive
   span = max(0.15*y0, 0.25*amp, 1e-8)
   lo=max(0.0, y0-span); hi=min(y_max, y0+span)
   if hi>lo:
      ref = np.linspace(lo,hi,25)
      eval similarly; combine.
   return cands/best values
```

```
def slsqp_active(a, time_limit, rng, K=12, maxiter=20):
    if not HAS_SCIPY: return a, seq_score(a)?
    a=normalize(a.copy()); b=full_conv(a); M=b.max(); score=2*a.size*M; best=a.copy(); best_score=score; best_M=M
    t0=time.monotonic(); outer=0
    while time... and outer<max_outer:
       # select active: top K indices by b plus any near max
       top_idx = np.argsort(b)[::-1][:K]
       # maybe add indices where b > M*0.95 to ensure, cap K2=20
       near = np.where(b > M*0.98)[0]
       active = np.unique(np.concatenate((top_idx,near)))
       if active.size > 20: active = top_idx  # cap
       x0 = np.concatenate([a, [M*0.95]])? Objective t lower? If t0=M, constraints active c≤M feasible. To enforce reduce, start t0=M*0.99? SLSQP may be infeasible. Start t0=M.
       t0=x[-1]=M*(1.0+1e-6)
       bounds = [(0,None)]*n + [(0, min(2.0, M*3+0.1))] maybe t upper. If t upper=M*1.01? Minimize t, no need upper for active? Use upper=None maybe. But if infeasible, t might shoot. Use upper = max(M*2, 0.1). M~0.006. Could also lower=0.
       try result = so.minimize(...)
       except: break
       if result.success: newa=result.x[:n] clip normalize; newM=max(full_conv(newa)); if newM<best_M: update.
       else maybe accept if finite and better.
       # if no improvement break? or active update
       outer+=1
    return best, score
```

Let's code helper functions carefully.

`full_conv`:
```
def full_conv(a):
    a = np.ascontiguousarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    size = 2*n - 1
    L = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    b = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)
    return b[:size]
```
For n maybe 0? Not.

`normalize_seq`:
```
def normalize_seq(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
       return np.full(a.size, 1.0/max(a.size,1))
    return a/s
```
If size 0? Not.

```
b=full_conv(a); s=a.sum(); if s<... return inf; return max(b)/(s*s)
```

```
def coord_line_search(a,b,S,j,cur_ratio,unaff_mask):

    x=a[j]
```

`coord_descent` details:
```
def coord_descent(a, time_limit, rng, verbose=False):
    a = normalize_seq(a)
    n=a.size
    b=full_conv(a); S=1.0; cur_M=float(np.max(b))
    best_a=a.copy(); best_M=cur_M
    masks=build_unaff_momasks(n)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    cycles=0; stall=0; noise=0.005
    time_limit=max(time_limit,0.5)
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
        order = rng.permutation(n)
        improved=False
        for j in order:
            if time.monotonic()-t0 > time_limit: break
            y, aff, r = coord_line_search(a,b,S,j,cur_M,masks[j])
            x = a[j]
            if r is not None and r < cur_M - 1e-14:
                # check finite
                if np.isfinite(y) and np.all(np.isfinite(aff)):
                    b[j:j+n]=aff
                    S = S - x + y
                    a[j]=y
                    cur_M = float(r)
                    improved=True
                    if cur_M < best_M:
                       best_M=cur_M; best_a=a.copy()
                    if S > 3.0 or S < 0.333333:
                       a = a/S; b = b/(S*S); S=1.0
                       cur_M = float(np.max(b)) # recompute to correct
                       # best_M should same; but due numerical
                       if cur_M < best_M: ...
        cycles +=1
        if not improved:
            stall +=1
            # perturb current around best
            # Use multiplicative? if current already best.
            a = normalize_seq(best_a + noise*rng.standard_normal(n))
            # maybe ensure not degenerate: mix with uniform
            a = 0.95*a + 0.05*(np.ones(n)/n)
            b=full_conv(a); S=1.0; cur_M=float(np.max(b))
            if cur_M < best_M:
               best_M=cur_M; best_a=a.copy()
            noise = min(0.25, noise*1.6) if stall>1 else max(0.002, noise*0.8?) 
            if stall > 25:
               break
        else:
            stall = 0
            # slight decrease noise? if improved
    return normalize_seq(best_a), float(best_M)
```
But if we perturb and become worse, cur_M > best_M. Coordinate line search compares to cur_M (worse), so it will accept moves that improve toward maybe best, good. It may get stuck in worse local; best preserved. `best_a` is best normalized. But `cur_M` should track current score; if after normalizing we set cur_M from b. Good.

Use `cand = best_a + noise*rng.standard_normal(n); cand = np.maximum(cand,0); cand += 1e-8; cand /= cand.sum()`. Good. Increase noise if repeated stalls. Start `noise=0.01`. After stall, noise *=1.5 up to 0.3. If improvement, reset noise to 0.01. This anneals.

We also can before each cycle occasionally do multiple perturbations if no improvement. Good.

We can further precompute self_mask per coordinate? `self_mask` length n with one True; creating 128 times per cycle (maybe 10M) waste. In `coord_line_search`, self_mask used in broadcasting; we can avoid boolean by adding self term separately:
`aff = block[None,:] + 2*delta*a[None,:]`

Careful `block` shape (n,), `delta` (m,1), `a` (n,) -> (m,n). Then `aff[:,j] += delta.ravel()**2`. But `delta` shape (m,1); `delta[:,0]**2`. We can compute `d2 = (y-x)**2`; `aff[:, j] += d2`. Good.

Need block current unnormalized. For self lag k=2j, formula base + 2δ a_j + δ²; since a at relative position j is x. `2δ*a` at j covers 2δx, plus δ². Good.

`aff = block[None, :] + 2.0 * delta * a[None, :]`; `aff[:, j] += (y - x)**2` (broadcast 1D). Then `M_abs = np.maximum(aff.max(axis=1), unaff_max)`. This is efficient.

`coord_line_search` arguments: `a,b,S,j,cur_M,mask`.
```
def coord_line_search(a, b, S, j, cur_M, mask):
    n = a.size
    x = a[j]
    block = b[j:j+n]
    if mask.any(): unaff = float(np.max(b[mask]))
    else: unaff = -1e300
    mean = S/n
    amp = max(mean, 0.25*math.sqrt(max(float(cur_M), 0.0)), 1e-8)
    y_max = max(S, 4.0*amp, 2.0*abs(x))
    y = make_y_candidates(x, S, cur_M, mean, y_max)
    if y.size ==0: return x, block, cur_M
    delta = y - x
    aff = block[None,:] + 2*delta[:,None]*a[None,:]
    aff[:, j] += delta*delta
    Mabs = np.maximum(aff, unaff).max(axis=1)
    Sy = S - x + y
    ratios = Mabs/(Sy*Sy)
    bad = ~np.isfinite(ratios) | (Sy<=0)
    ratios[bad] = np.inf
    imin = int(np.argmin(ratios))
    y_best = y[imin]; r_best=ratios[imin]; aff_best=aff[imin]
    # refine
    span = max(0.15*y_best, 0.3*amp, 1e-8)
    lo = max(0.0, y_best - span)
    hi = min(y_max, y_best + span)
    if hi > lo:
        ref = np.linspace(lo, hi, 15)
        # add boundaries/best
        ref = np.unique(np.concatenate((ref, np.array([lo, hi]) )))
        rd = ref - x
        raff = block[None,:] + 2*rd[:,None]*a[None,:]
        raff[:, j] += rd*rd
        rM = np.maximum(raff, unaff).max(axis=1)
        rSy = S - x + ref
        rr = rM/(rSy*rSy); bad ...
        j2=argmin; if rr[j2]<r_best: update
    return float(y_best), aff_best.astype(np.float64), float(r_best)
```
`np.maximum(aff, unaff)` with unaff scalar works.

Make candidates top-level:
```
def make_y_candidates(x,S,cur_M,mean,y_max):
   amp = max(mean, 0.25*math.sqrt(max(float(cur_M),0.0)), 1e-8)
   # if y_max < 0: return [x]
   parts = [np.array([0.0, x])]
   if x>0: parts.append(x*COORD_X_MULTS)
   parts.append(amp*COORD_ABS_MULTS)
   parts.append(S*COORD_SUM_MULTS)
   y = np.concatenate(parts)
   y = y[np.isfinite(y) & (y>=0) & (y<=y_max)]
   # ensure y includes x clipped, 0
   if 0 <= x <= y_max: pass else add? x should.
   if y.size > 50: y = y[:50]? unique
   # unique then maybe add linspace if size <15
   y = np.unique(y)
   if y.size < 15: y=np.unique(np.concatenate((y,np.linspace(0.0,y_max,15))))
   return y
```

But adding many SLSQP function definitions is okay. Need no closures. Let's write:
```
def slsqp_obj(x, n):
    return x[-1]

def slsqp_obj_jac(x, n):
    v=np.zeros(x.size); v[-1]=1.0; return v

def slsqp_eq(x, n):
    return np.sum(x[:n]) - 1.0

def slsqp_eq_jac(x, n):
    J=np.zeros((1,x.size)); J[0,:n]=1.0; return J

def slsqp_ineq(x, active, n):
    a=x[:n]; t=x[-1]; out=np.empty(active.size)
    for idx,k in enumerate(active):
       k=int(k)
       lo=max(0, k-(n-1)); hi=min(n-1, k)
       if lo>hi:
          out[idx]=t
       else:
          i1=np.arange(lo, hi+1)
          i2=k-i1
          out[idx]=t - float(np.dot(a[i1], a[i2]))
    return out

def slsqp_ineq_jac(x, active, n):
    a=x[:n]; m=active.size; J=np.zeros((m, n+1))
    for idx,k in enumerate(active):
       k=int(k)
       js = k - np.arange(n)
       valid = (js >= 0) & (js < n)
       if np.any(valid):
          J[idx, valid] = -2.0 * a[js[valid]]
       J[idx, -1] = 1.0
    return J
```
Potential negative constraints if t < c. Good.

`slsqp_active`:
```
def slsqp_active(a, time_limit, rng, K=10, max_outer=50):
   if not HAS_MINIMIZE: return normalize(a), get_M
   a=normalize; b=full_conv; M=b.max(); best...
   t0...
   outer=0
   Kcur=8
   while time... outer<max_outer:
      order = np.argsort(b)[::-1]
      active = order[:Kcur]
      # add near max maybe cap 14
      near = np.where(b > M*0.98)[0]
      active = np.sort(np.unique(np.concatenate((active,near))))
      if active.size > 16: active=order[:16]
      x0 = np.concatenate((a, [M*(1+1e-6)]))
      bounds = [(0.0, None)]*n + [(0.0, 2.0)]  # t upper 2
      eq = {'type':'eq','fun':slsqp_eq,'jac':slsqp_eq_jac,'args':(n,)}
      ineq = {'type':'ineq','fun':slsqp_ineq,'jac':slsqp_ineq_jac,'args':(active,n)}
      try: res = so.minimize(slsqp_obj, x0, args=(n,), method='SLSQP', jac=slsqp_obj_jac, bounds=bounds, constraints=[eq, ineq], options={'maxiter':30,'ftol':1e-10,'disp':False})
      except Exception: res=None
      if res is not None and res.x is not None:
          na = np.asarray(res.x[:n]); na=np.maximum(na,0); S=na.sum(); if S>0: na/=S
          nb=full_conv(na); newM=nb.max()
          if newM < best_M - 1e-13:
              best_M=newM; best_a=na; a=best_a.copy(); b=nb; updated=True
          # maybe if res.x[-1] < M? even if actual not better, update a? Could accept if newM < M? do above.
          if newM < M: M=newM; b=nb; a=best? but best.
          else: if no improve, maybe break after few? 
      else: break?
      outer+=1
      # slightly increase K if no improve
      if outer % 3 ==0: Kcur = min(16, Kcur+2)
   return best normalized,best_M
```
But SLSQP may take >time; we check before call and maybe maxiter low. It might return infeasible newM worse. We only accept better. If no improvement after e.g. 4 inits/active, break to save time. But budget small.

```
def optimize_start(a, time_limit, rng, use_lp=True, use_slsqp=True):
   if time_limit <=0: return normalize(a), get_M
   a=normalize(a); M=seq_M(a); best_a=a; best_M=M
    tstart=time.monotonic()
   # phase 1 coordinate 50%
   tb1=min(time_limit*0.5, time_limit)
   a1,M1=coord_descent(a, tb1, rng)
   if M1<best_M: update
   # if time remain
   time_left = time_limit - (time.monotonic()-tstart)
   if use_lp and HAS_LINPROG and time_left>5:
       tb2=min(time_left*0.25, max(5, time_limit*0.2))
       a2,M2=lp_active_move(a1,tb2,rng)
       if M2<best_M: update
       time_left = ...
   if use_slsqp and HAS_MINIMIZE and time_left>5:
       tb3=min(time_left*0.2, max(5, time_limit*0.15))
       a3,M3=slsqp_active(a1,tb3,rng)
       update
   # final coordinate
   if time_left>3:
       a4,M4=coord_descent(best_a, min(time_left, max(3, time_limit*0.2)), rng)
       update
   return normalize(best_a), best_M
```

```
    inits=[]

    # U powers
    for p in (-0.5? lower edges? Actually m^-p)
       a = m**(-p) ; for p in [0,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0,1.5]
       add noise r=0.01*abs(normal)
    # Two-sided power low? For U shape p positive.
    # Edge decay: a = exp(-alpha*m/n) maybe lower center? Actually exp(-alpha*m/n) high edges; alpha in [0,0.5,1,2,4]
    # One-sided powers: a=(i+1)^-p and (n-i)^-p, mix
```

Now, let's flesh out each function with robust code.

`full_conv`:
```
def full_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([0.0])
    size = 2*n - 1
    L = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    b = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)
    return b[:size]
```
For n=1, size1, L1, b length1.

`normalize_seq`:
```
def normalize_seq(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size
    return a / s
```
If all zeros, uniform.

`seq_M`:
```
def seq_M(a):
    a = normalize_seq(a)
    b = full_conv(a)
    return float(np.max(b))
```

`build_unaff_momasks`:
```
def build_unaff_momasks(n):
    m = 2*n - 1
    masks = []
    for j in range(n):
        mask = np.ones(m, dtype=bool)
        mask[j:j+n] = False
        masks.append(mask)
    return masks
```
For n large maybe memory, okay.

`make_y_candidates`:
```
def make_y_candidates(x, S, cur_M, mean_scale, y_max):
    x = float(x); S=float(S); cur_M=float(cur_M)
    amp = max(mean_scale, 0.25*math.sqrt(max(cur_M, 0.0)), 1e-8)
    y_max = max(float(y_max), amp, 1e-12)
    parts = [np.array([0.0], dtype=float)]
    if 0.0 <= x <= y_max:
       parts.append(np.array([x], dtype=float))
    if x > 0:
       parts.append(x * COORD_X_MULTS)
    parts.append(amp * COORD_ABS_MULTS)
    parts.append(S * COORD_SUM_MULTS)
    y = np.concatenate(parts)
    y = y[np.isfinite(y) & (y >= 0.0) & (y <= y_max)]
    if y.size == 0:
       y = np.array([0.0])
    # If x outside? add clipped
    if x <= y_max:
       y = np.unique(np.concatenate((y, np.array([x]))))
    else:
       y = np.unique(np.concatenate((y, np.array([y_max]))))
    if y.size < 12:
       y = np.unique(np.concatenate((y, np.linspace(0.0, y_max, 12))))
    # Limit size to prevent blow
    if y.size > 40:
       # keep every? We can unique already. If too many, sample evenly?
       idx = np.linspace(0, y.size-1, 30, dtype=int)
       y = y[idx]
    return y
```
But if x > y_max impossible unless S changed? x≤S≤y_max? y_max≥S. Good.

COORD arrays: include 1.0.

`coord_line_search`:
```
def coord_line_search(a, b, S, j, cur_M, mask):
    n = a.size
    x = float(a[j])
    block = b[j:j+n]
    if mask is not None:
       if mask.any(): unaff = float(np.max(b[mask]))
       else: unaff = -1e300
    else:
       # compute outside
       if n == 1: unaff = -1e300
       else:
          if j > 0 and (2*n-2) >= (j+n):
             unaff = max(float(np.max(b[:j])), float(np.max(b[j+n:])))
          elif j > 0: unaff = float(np.max(b[:j]))
          else: unaff = -1e300? Wait if j=0 block first n, outside suffix b[n:], length n-1. if j+n <=2n-1 yes. so max.
    mean = S / max(n,1)
    amp = max(mean, 0.25*math.sqrt(max(float(cur_M),0.0)), 1e-8)
    y_max = max(S, 4.0*amp, 2.0*abs(x))
    y = make_y_candidates(x, S, cur_M, mean, y_max)
    if y.size == 0:
       return x, block.astype(float), float(cur_M)
    delta = y - x
    aff = block[None, :] + 2.0 * delta[:, None] * a[None, :]
    aff[:, j] += delta * delta
    Mabs = np.maximum(aff, unaff)
    Mabs = Mabs.max(axis=1)
    Sy = S - x + y
    ratios = Mabs / (Sy * Sy)
    bad = (~np.isfinite(ratios)) | (Sy <= 0.0) | (Mabs < -1e-15)
    if np.any(bad): ratios[bad] = np.inf
    imin = int(np.argmin(ratios))
    y_best = float(y[imin]); r_best = float(ratios[imin]); aff_best = aff[imin].copy()
    if np.isfinite(r_best):
       span = max(0.15 * y_best, 0.3 * amp, 1e-8)
       lo = max(0.0, y_best - span)
       hi = min(y_max, y_best + span)
       if hi > lo:
          ref = np.linspace(lo, hi, 15)
          ref = np.unique(np.concatenate((ref, np.array([lo, hi]) )))
          rd = ref - x
          raff = block[None, :] + 2.0 * rd[:, None] * a[None, :]
          raff[:, j] += rd * rd
          rM = np.maximum(raff, unaff).max(axis=1)
          rSy = S - x + ref
          rr = rM / (rSy * rSy)
          bad2 = (~np.isfinite(rr)) | (rSy <= 0.0) | (rM < -1e-15)
          if np.any(bad2): rr[bad2]=np.inf
          j2 = int(np.argmin(rr))
          if rr[j2] < r_best:
             r_best=float(rr[j2]); y_best=float(ref[j2]); aff_best=raff[j2].copy()
    return y_best, aff_best, r_best
```
Note in `unaff` computation when mask None. Let's implement simpler for safety:
```
if mask is None:
    outside = b.copy()
    outside[j:j+n] = -1e300
    unaff = float(np.max(outside))
```
This O(n) copy per coord for large only; but for n=128, we use mask. In coord_descent if n>1024 masks maybe too large? We can still build masks for n<=512 else None. Use None fallback. Good.

But in mask case, `mask.any()` always true for n>1, outside length n-1; for n=1, false. Good.

`coord_descent` code:
```
def coord_descent(a, time_limit, rng):
    if time_limit <= 0:
        a=normalize_seq(a); return a, seq_M(a)
    a = normalize_seq(a)
    n=a.size
    if n == 1:
       return a, 1.0 # score M? For a [1], conv max1
    b=full_conv(a); S=1.0; cur_M=float(np.max(b))
    best_a=a.copy(); best_M=cur_M
    if n <= 512:
        masks = build_unaff_momasks(n)
    else:
        masks = None
    t0=time.monotonic()
    time_limit=max(time_limit, 1.0)
    cycles=0; stall=0; noise=0.01
    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
       order = rng.permutation(n)
       improved=False
       for j in order:
          if time.monotonic() - t0 > time_limit: break
          mask = masks[j] if masks is not None else None
          y, aff, r = coord_line_search(a,b,S,j,cur_M,mask)
          x = float(a[j])
          if np.isfinite(r) and r < cur_M - 1e-14 and y >= 0.0:
             b[j:j+n] = aff
             S = S - x + y
             a[j] = y
             cur_M = float(r)
             improved = True
             if cur_M < best_M - 1e-15:
                best_M = cur_M
                best_a = a.copy()
             if S > 3.0 or S < 0.3333333333333333:
                a = a / S
                b = b / (S * S)
                S = 1.0
                cur_M = float(np.max(b))
                if cur_M < best_M - 1e-15:
                   best_M = cur_M; best_a = a.copy()
       cycles += 1
       if not improved:
          stall += 1
          # perturb from best
          cand = normalize_seq(best_a) + noise * rng.standard_normal(n)
          cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
          cand += 1e-12
          cand = cand / cand.sum()
          b = full_conv(cand)
          S = 1.0
          cur_M = float(np.max(b))
          a = cand
          if cur_M < best_M - 1e-15:
             best_M=cur_M; best_a=a.copy()
          if stall == 1:
             noise = 0.01
          else:
             noise = min(0.35, noise * 1.5)
          if stall >= 35 and noise > 0.3:
             break
       else:
          stall = 0
          if noise > 0.02:
             noise = max(0.01, noise * 0.8)
    return normalize_seq(best_a), float(best_M)
```
Need if `noise * standard_normal(n)` may create negative values, clip. Good.

Potential issue: If `stall` increases, `cur_M` becomes worse than best. Next cycles coordinate line search compared to cur_M, can move to best local maybe but not necessarily better than best. If no improvement over display? It might get stuck in worsened local. On next stall, noise increases. Good.

But if current a is worse than best, and coordinate moves improve current to a value still worse than best, we update `cur_M` but not best. `best_a` unchanged. If many cycles no improvement over best but current improves, `improved` True (relative cur_M) so stall reset and noise decreases; this may prevent trying larger noise. Good because it's working. However it may stay in basin not best. Coordinate from worse might not reach best due only improvements; but eventually local. When no further improvement, stall -> perturb best again. Fine.

Potential issue: `improved` True if any coordinate improves relative current, even if current far worse. Good.

Now `lp_active_move`. Need use `full_conv` after each accepted. It normalizes a sum1. We can also use masks? No.
```
def lp_active_move(a, time_limit, rng):
    if not HAS_LINPROG or time_limit <= 0:
       a=normalize_seq(a); return a, seq_M(a)
    a=normalize_seq(a); n=a.size
    b=full_conv(a); S=1; M=float(np.max(b))
    best_a=a.copy(); best_M=M
    t0=time.monotonic(); fail=0; iter_count=0
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
       if M <= 0: break
       order = np.argsort(b)[::-1]
       Kbase = 10 + (iter_count % 4) * 2
       K = min(n? no, b.size, Kbase)
       active = order[:K]
       if b.size > K + 2:
           extra_start = rng.integers(K, min(80, b.size))
           active = np.concatenate((active, order[extra_start], order[extra_start+1 if ...]))
       active = np.unique(active)
       found=False
       for delta in (0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02):
          A_ub = np.zeros((active.size, n+1))
          b_ub = np.zeros(active.size)
          for r,k in enumerate(active):
              k=int(k)
              js = k - np.arange(n)
              valid = (js>=0)&(js<n)
              if np.any(valid): A_ub[r, valid] = 2.0 * a[js[valid]]
              A_ub[r,-1] = -1.0
              b_ub[r] = -float(b[k])
          c_obj = np.zeros(n+1); c_obj[-1]=1.0
          low = -np.maximum(a, 0.0) * (1.0 + 1e-12)
          high = np.full(n, delta)
          bounds = list(zip(low, high)); bounds.append((0.0, None))
          A_eq = np.zeros(n+1); A_eq[:n]=1.0
          try:
             res = _linprog(c_obj, A_ub, b_ub, A_eq, 0.0, bounds=bounds, method='highs')
          except Exception:
             res=None
          if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x') and res.x is not None:
             d = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=float)
             if np.all(np.isfinite(d)) and np.linalg.norm(d) > 1e-12:
                best_alpha=None; best_newM=M
                for alpha in (1.0,0.7,0.5,0.3,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02):
                   na = a + alpha*d
                   # clip tiny negative
                   if na.min() < -1e-9:
                      # if significant, skip
                      if na.min() < -1e-7: continue
                   na = np.maximum(na,0.0)
                   s=na.sum()
                   if s<=0: continue
                   na = na/s
                   nb=full_conv(na); newM=float(np.max(nb))
                   if newM < best_newM - 1e-14:
                      best_newM=newM; best_alpha=alpha
                if best_alpha is not None and best_newM < best_M - 1e-14:
                   na = np.maximum(a + best_alpha*d,0); na/=na.sum()
                   b=full_conv(na); a=na; M=best_newM
                   if M < best_M - 1e-14:
                       best_M=M; best_a=a.copy()
                   found=True
                   break
          iter_count +=1
       if found:
          fail=0
       else:
          fail +=1
          if fail >= 25:
             break
       iter_count += 1
    return normalize_seq(best_a), float(best_M)
```
Need if t_opt not checked? We just use d if norm>0. But LP could choose direction not improving due second-order; line search fails. Good.

We can also use `res.fun`? Not needed. However if objective t minimized and t close >M due constraints? d may not reduce all. Line search catches.

Maybe require `res.status == 0` or `res.fun < M`? We could if `res.status !=0` skip, but if x exists maybe still. Use status.
```
if res is not None and res.status==0 and ...
```
But if status 1 with good? Use status in (0,1). However infeasible (3) maybe no. Use `res.status in (0,1)`.

`slsqp_active`:
```
def slsqp_active(a, time_limit, rng):
    a=normalize_seq(a); n=a.size
    b=full_conv(a); M=float(np.max(b)); best_a=a.copy(); best_M=M
    if not HAS_SCIPY_OPT or time_limit <= 5: return ...
    t0=time.monotonic(); outer=0; K=8; fail=0
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit and outer<40:
       if M <= 0: break
       order = np.argsort(b)[::-1]
       active = order[:K]
       near = np.where(b > M*0.98)[0]
       active = np.unique(np.concatenate((active,near)))
       if active.size > 16: active = order[:16]
       x0 = np.concatenate((a, [M*(1.0+1e-6)]))
       bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n + [(0.0, 2.0)]
       eq = {'type':'eq','fun':slsqp_eq,'jac':slsqp_eq_jac,'args':(n,)}
       ineq = {'type':'ineq','fun':slsqp_ineq,'jac':slsqp_ineq_jac,'args':(active,n)}
       try:
          res = _so.minimize(slsqp_obj, x0, args=(n,), method='SLSQP', jac=slsqp_obj_jac, bounds=bounds, constraints=[eq, ineq], options={'maxiter':25, 'ftol':1e-10, 'disp':False})
       except Exception:
          res=None
       updated=False
       if res is not None and getattr(res, 'x', None) is not None:
          na = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=float)
          na = np.where(np.isfinite(na), na, 0.0); na=np.maximum(na,0)
          s=na.sum()
          if s > 1e-300:
             na /= s
             nb=full_conv(na); newM=float(np.max(nb))
             if newM < best_M - 1e-14:
                best_M=newM; best_a=na.copy(); a=na.copy(); b=nb; M=newM; updated=True
             elif newM < M - 1e-14: # even if not best, accept current to continue
                a=na.copy(); b=nb; M=newM; # but keep best separate
          # If result t lower? Maybe accept even if not better actual? no.
       if not updated:
          fail +=1
          if fail > 6: break
       else:
          fail=0; K = min(16, K+2)
       outer +=1
    return normalize_seq(best_a), float(best_M)
```
Potential issue: If SLSQP result current a improves from M but not best, we don't accept unless <best? We can accept if newM < M to continue local, as code last elif. But if newM < M only slightly, good. If not, skip. 

`bounds`: t upper 2.0. For normalized M ≤1? Is max conv ≤1? Sum a=1 and nonneg, c0≤1, center≤? Max ≤1 (can be 1 if one coordinate 1). So 2 enough. If n large maybe c max ≤ norm? Sum1, c_k≤(sum)^2/4? For nonnegative, each c_k ≤ (sum of subset)^2? Max ≤1. So 2 ok.

```
def generate_inits(n, rng):
    inits=[]
    i=np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    center = (n-1)/2.0
    m = np.minimum(np.abs(i-center)+? Actually distance to nearest edge: min(i, n-1-i)
    d = np.minimum(i, n-1-i)
    # U powers with additive c to avoid singular
    for p in (-0.5,0.0,0.2,0.5,0.75,1.0,1.5):
       a = (d+1.0)**(-p) + 1e-6
       if p<0: ??? p negative gives edges? (d+1)^0.5 low center? We'll use positive for high edges.
       noise = 0.001*max(a.max(),1)*abs(rng.standard_normal(n))
       inits.append(normalize_seq(a+noise))
    # exponential edges
    for lam in (0.1,0.5,1.0,2.0,5.0):
       a=np.exp(-lam*(d/max(n,1))) + 1e-6
       inits.append(normalize_seq(a))
    # one-sided powers
    for p in (0.5,1.0,2.0):
       a=(i+1.0)**(-p) + 1e-6
       inits.append(normalize_seq(a))
       a=(n-i)**(-p)+1e-6
       inits.append(normalize_seq(a))
    # cosine
    for f in (1,2,3,4,6,8):
       if n>2:
          a=(1.0+np.cos(2π f i/(n-1)))**2 +1e-6
          inits.append(normalize_seq(a))
       a=(1.0-np.cos(...))**2 +1e-6 # anti
    # cluster gaussians
    for _ in range(4):
       k = 2 + rng.integers(0,2)
       centers = rng.uniform(0,n,k)
       sigma = n*(0.03+0.1*rng.random())
       a=np.zeros(n)
       for c in centers:
          a += np.exp(-((i-c)/sigma)**2)
       a += 0.01*abs(rng.standard_normal(n)); inits.append(normalize_seq(a))
    # random smooth:
    for _ in range(2):
       a = 1.0 + 0.5*rng.standard_normal(n)
       inits.append(normalize_seq(a))
    for _ in range(2):
       a = np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n)) + 0.01
       inits.append(normalize_seq(a))
    # uniform/noisy
    a=np.ones(n)+0.1*rng.standard_normal(n); inits.append(normalize_seq(a))
    return inits
```
Need top-level `normalize_seq` can handle negative noise by max. Good.

This generates many inits ~30; we can score and sort. For n=128, ok. To reduce, maybe fewer. We'll limit final starts to 12.

Need ensure clusters centers in [0,n-1]. `rng.uniform(0,n,k)` okay.

Code:
```
def dual_lp_move(a, time_limit, rng):
    if not HAS_LINPROG or time_limit <= 0: return...
    a=normalize; b=full; M=max; best...
    t0; iter=0; fail=0
    U=4.0
    while time...:
       lam_list = [1.0, 0.98, 0.95, 0.9]
       found=False
       for lam in lam_list:
          if lam*M <= 0: continue
          # build A
          A=np.zeros((b.size,n)); 
          for k in range(b.size):
             lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
             if lo<=hi:
                idx=np.arange(lo,hi+1)
                A[k,idx] = a[k-idx]
          c_obj=-np.ones(n)
          bounds=[(0.0,U)]*n
          try: res=_linprog(c_obj,A,b.size*M*lam,bounds=bounds,method='highs')
          except: res=None
          if res is not None and res.status==0:
             g=np.asarray(res.x); s=g.sum()
             if s>1e-15:
                g=g/s
                tg=M_dual? seqM(g)
                if seqM(g) < best_M: update g
                best_alpha=None; best_newM=best_M
                for alpha in (0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.75,1.0):
                   na=(1-alpha)*a + alpha*g; na=np.maximum(na,0); s=na.sum(); if s<1e-300 continue; na/=s
                   nb=full; newM=max
                   if newM < best_newM - 1e-14: best_newM=newM; best_alpha=alpha
                if best_alpha is not None and best_newM < best_M -1e-14:
                    # accept
                    na...; a=na; b=nb; M=best_newM; best...
                    found=True
                    break
          iter_count...
       if found: fail=0
       else:
          fail+=1
          # slightly perturb a for next LP based on best maybe
          if fail>5: break
          a_try = normalize_seq(best_a + 0.005*rng.standard_normal(n))
          # keep current a? maybe set a=a_try for LP search but best unchanged
          a = a_try; b=full_conv(a); M=max(b)
       # optionally if many, random noise
    return
```
But perturbing `a` to worse can LP from worse; best remains. It may find improvement. Good.

Now, code `optimize_start` code directly:
```
def optimize_start(a, time_limit, rng, use_lp=True, use_slsqp=True):
    if time_limit <= 0:
       a = normalize_seq(a); return a, seq_M(a)
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    a = normalize_seq(a)
    M = seq_M(a)
    best_a = a.copy(); best_M = M
    # coordinate phase
    tb1 = time_limit * 0.5
    a1, M1 = coord_descent(a, tb1, rng)
    if M1 < best_M:
       best_M = M1; best_a = a1.copy()
    elapsed = time.monotonic()-t_start
    rem = time_limit - elapsed
    current = best_a.copy()
    if use_lp and HAS_LINPROG and rem > 8:
       tb2 = min(rem * 0.30, time_limit * 0.20, 180.0)
       a2, M2 = lp_active_move(current, tb2, rng)
       if M2 < best_M:
          best_M=M2; best_a=a2.copy()
       rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic()-t_start)
       current = best_a.copy()
       if rem > 8:
          tb2b = min(rem * 0.30, time_limit * 0.20, 180.0)
          a2, M2 = dual_lp_move(current, tb2b, rng)
          if M2 < best_M: update
          rem=...
    if use_slsqp and HAS_SCIPY_OPT and rem > 5:
       tb3 = min(rem * 0.4, 20.0)
       a3,M3 = slsqp_active(best_a.tb3,rng)
       update
       rem=...
    if rem - 1 > 3:
       tb4 = min(rem - 1.0, max(3.0, time_limit*0.25))
       a4,M4 = coord_descent(best_a,tb4,rng)
       update
    return normalize_seq(best_a), float(best_M)
```
Need ensure `tb2` not negative. Use `max(0.0, ...)`.

If time_limit small, tb1 = time_limit*0.5; coordinate returns. rem maybe small. final possibly if rem>3.

```
def build_dual_rows(n):
    rows=[]; m=2*n-1
    for k in range(m):
       lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
       if lo<=hi:
          rows.append((k, np.arange(lo,hi+1, dtype=int)))
       else:
          rows.append((k, None))
    return rows
```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(10.0, float(budget_s) - 30.0)
    prev_a = None
    n_target = 128
    try:
       h = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
       h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
       h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
       if h.size >= 2 and h.sum() > 1e-9:
          prev_a = h
          n_target = int(h.size)
    except Exception:
       prev_a = None
    if n_target < 2:
       n_target = 128
    best_a = None; best_M = np.inf
    if prev_a is not None:
       a = normalize_seq(prev_a)
       m = seq_M(a)
       if m < np.inf:
          best_a = a.copy(); best_M = m
       print(...)
    # generate inits
    inits = generate_inits(n_target, rng)
    inits_scored=[]
    for idx,a in enumerate(inits):
       m=seq_M(a)
       if np.isfinite(m): inits_scored.append((m, idx, a))
    inits_scored.sort()
    if best_a is None and inits_scored:
       m0,_,a0 = inits_scored[0]
       best_a=a0.copy(); best_M=m0
       print(...)
    # budgets
    total = max(60.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if prev_a is not None:
       primary_frac=0.55; explore_frac=0.30; final_frac=0.15
    else:
       primary_frac=0.35; explore_frac=0.45; final_frac=0.20
    # primary
    if best_a is not None:
       primary_budget = total * primary_frac
       remain = deadline - time.monotonic() - 10.0
       tb = min(primary_budget, remain)
       if tb > 5:
          a_p, m_p = optimize_start(best_a, tb, rng, True, True)
          if m_p < best_M:
             best_M=m_p; best_a=a_p.copy(); print
          elif a_p.size == n_target:
             # if equal or worse, keep best but maybe if m_p close lower by num? no
             pass
    # explore
    if n_target <= 256: max_explore=8
    elif n_target <= 1024: max_explore=4
    else: max_explore=2
    pool = inits_scored if prev_a is not None else inits_scored[1:]
    pool = pool[:max_explore]
    if pool and best_a is not None:
       explore_budget = max(60.0, total * explore_frac)
       per = explore_budget / max(1, len(pool))
       for item in pool:
          m_i, idx_i, a_i = item
          remain = deadline - time.monotonic() - 5.0
          if remain < 15: break
          tb = per
          # cap and reduce if bad
          tb = min(tb, remain * 0.7, 240.0)
          if m_i > best_M * 3.0: tb *= 0.25
          elif m_i > best_M * 1.5: tb *= 0.5
          if tb > 8:
              use_lp = tb > 40 and m_i < best_M * 1.3
              a_e, m_e = optimize_start(a_i, tb, rng, use_lp, False)
              if m_e < best_M:
                 best_M=m_e; best_a=a_e.copy(); print
    # final
    if best_a is not None:
       remain = deadline - time.monotonic() - 5.0
       final_budget = max(30.0, total * final_frac)
       tb = min(final_budget, remain)
       if tb > 5:
          a_f, m_f = optimize_start(best_a, tb, rng, True, True)
          if m_f < best_M:
             best_M=m_f; best_a=a_f.copy(); print
    # fallback
    if best_a is None:
       best_a = np.ones(n_target, dtype=float)/n_target
       best_M = seq_M(best_a)
    arr = normalize_seq(best_a)
    arr = arr + 1e-12
    arr = arr / arr.sum()
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    result = [float(x) for x in arr]
    try: final_eval = float(evaluate_sequence(result))
    except Exception: final_eval = float('inf')
    print(...)
    return result
```

`generate_inits` code:
```
def generate_inits(n, rng):
    inits=[]
    i=np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    d=np.minimum(i, n-1-i)
    def? no nested. Use local variables.
    if n <= 512:
       for p in (-0.5, 0.0, 0.2, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.5):
          a = (d + 1.0) ** (-p) + 1e-8
          a = a + 0.002 * np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n))
          inits.append(normalize_seq(a))
       for lam in (0.1,0.5,1.0,2.0,5.0):
          a = np.exp(-lam * (d / max(float(n),1.0))) + 1e-8
          inits.append(normalize_seq(a))
       for p in (0.5,1.0,2.0):
          a = (i + 1.0) ** (-p) + 1e-8
          inits.append(normalize_seq(a))
          a = (n - i) ** (-p) + 1e-8
          inits.append(normalize_seq(a))
       if n > 2:
          denom = float(n - 1)
          for f in (1,2,3,4,6,8):
              a = (1.0 + np.cos(2.0*math.pi*f*i/denom)) ** 2 + 1e-8
              inits.append(normalize_seq(a))
              a = (1.0 - np.cos(2.0*math.pi*f*i/denom)) ** 2 + 1e-8
              inits.append(normalize_seq(a))
       for _ in range(3):
          k = 2 + int(rng.integers(0, 2, dtype=int)? rng.integers(0,2) returns 0/1)
          centers = rng.uniform(0.0, float(n), size=k)
          a = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
          for c in centers:
             sigma = max(1.0, float(n)*(0.03 + 0.10*float(rng.random())))
             a += np.exp(-((i-c)/sigma)**2)
          a += 0.01 * np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n))
          inits.append(normalize_seq(a))
       for _ in range(2):
          a = 1.0 + 0.4*rng.standard_normal(n)
          inits.append(normalize_seq(a))
       for _ in range(2):
          a = np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n)) + 0.01
          inits.append(normalize_seq(a))
    else:
       # simple
       a = np.ones(n); inits.append(normalize_seq(a))
       a = (d+1.0)**(-0.5)+1e-8; inits.append(normalize_seq(a))
       a = (d+1.0)**(-1.0)+1e-8; inits.append(normalize_seq(a))
       a = 1+0.5*rng.standard_normal(n); inits.append(normalize_seq(a))
    if not inits:
       inits.append(np.ones(n, dtype=float)/n)
    return inits
```

```
    else: mx1 = -1e300
    end = j+n
    if end < b.size:
        mx2 = float(np.max(b[end:]))

```

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative sequence of fixed length minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a))/sum(a)^2.

The method combines:
- fast exact one-coordinate descent in the scale-invariant objective,
- active-set LP directions for the maximum-convolution objective,
- SOTA-style dual LP direction (linear constraint |a*g| <= M),
- small SLSQP active-set polishing,
- diverse initial profiles and global restarts.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    import scipy.optimize as _so
    HAS_SCIPY_OPT = True
except Exception:
    _so = None
    HAS_SCIPY_OPT = False

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog as _linprog
    HAS_LINPROG = True
except Exception:
    _linprog = None
    HAS_LINPROG = False


COORD_X_MULTS = np.array(
    [0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.25, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0, 5.0],
    dtype=np.float64
)

COORD_ABS_MULTS = np.array(
    [0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 8.0],
    dtype=np.float64
)

COORD_SUM_MULTS = np.array(
    [0.005, 0.01, 0.025, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.4, 0.7, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0],
    dtype=np.float64
)


def full_conv(a):
    """Full linear convolution a*a via FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    L = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    b = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)
    return b[:size]


def normalize_seq(a):
    """Clip to non-negative finite values and normalize sum to 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def seq_M(a):
    """Normalized maximum convolution coefficient, i.e. score/(2n)."""
    a = normalize_seq(a)
    b = full_conv(a)
    return float(np.max(b))


def build_unaff_momasks(n):
    """Boolean masks of convolution indices not affected by coordinate j."""
    m = 2 * n - 1
    masks = []
    for j in range(n):
        mask = np.ones(m, dtype=bool)
        mask[j:j + n] = False
        masks.append(mask)
    return masks


def make_y_candidates(x, S, cur_M, mean_scale, y_max):
    """Candidate values for one-coordinate update."""
    x = float(x)
    S = float(S)
    cur_M = float(cur_M)
    amp = max(float(mean_scale), 0.25 * math.sqrt(max(cur_M, 0.0)), 1e-8)
    y_max = max(float(y_max), amp, 1e-12)

    parts = [np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)]
    if 0.0 <= x <= y_max:
        parts.append(np.array([x], dtype=np.float64))
    if x > 0.0:
        parts.append(x * COORD_X_MULTS)
    parts.append(amp * COORD_ABS_MULTS)
    parts.append(S * COORD_SUM_MULTS)

    y = np.concatenate(parts)
    y = y[np.isfinite(y) & (y >= 0.0) & (y <= y_max)]
    if y.size == 0:
        y = np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)

    if x <= y_max:
        y = np.unique(np.concatenate((y, np.array([x], dtype=np.float64))))
    else:
        y = np.unique(np.concatenate((y, np.array([y_max], dtype=np.float64))))

    if y.size < 14:
        y = np.unique(np.concatenate((y, np.linspace(0.0, y_max, 14))))

    if y.size > 42:
        idx = np.linspace(0, y.size - 1, 32, dtype=int)
        y = np.unique(y[idx])

    return y


def coord_line_search(a, b, S, j, cur_M, mask):
    """Exact/numerical 1-D line search for coordinate j."""
    n = a.size
    x = float(a[j])
    block = b[j:j + n]

    if mask is not None:
        if mask.any():
            unaff = float(np.max(b[mask]))
        else:
            unaff = -1e300
    else:
        mx1 = float(np.max(b[:j])) if j > 0 else -1e300
        end = j + n
        mx2 = float(np.max(b[end:])) if end < b.size else -1e300
        unaff = mx1 if mx1 >= mx2 else mx2

    mean = float(S) / float(n)
    amp = max(mean, 0.25 * math.sqrt(max(float(cur_M), 0.0)), 1e-8)
    y_max = max(float(S), 8.0 * amp, 2.0 * abs(x))

    y = make_y_candidates(x, S, cur_M, mean, y_max)
    if y.size == 0:
        return x, block.astype(np.float64), float(cur_M)

    delta = y - x
    aff = block[None, :] + 2.0 * delta[:, None] * a[None, :]
    aff[:, j] += delta * delta

    Mabs = np.maximum(aff, unaff).max(axis=1)
    Sy = S - x + y
    ratios = Mabs / (Sy * Sy)
    bad = (~np.isfinite(ratios)) | (Sy <= 0.0) | (Mabs < -1e-15)
    if np.any(bad):
        ratios[bad] = np.inf

    order_y = np.argsort(ratios)
    imin = int(order_y[0])
    y_best = float(y[imin])
    r_best = float(ratios[imin])
    aff_best = aff[imin].copy()

    chosen = [imin]
    if order_y.size > 1:
        second = int(order_y[1])
        if second != imin:
            chosen.append(second)

    for ci in chosen:
        y0 = float(y[ci])
        span = max(0.15 * y0, 0.3 * amp, 1e-8)
        lo = max(0.0, y0 - span)
        hi = min(y_max, y0 + span)
        if hi <= lo:
            continue

        ref_points = 13 if n <= 256 else 9
        ref = np.linspace(lo, hi, ref_points)
        ref = np.unique(np.concatenate((ref, np.array([lo, hi], dtype=np.float64))))

        rd = ref - x
        raff = block[None, :] + 2.0 * rd[:, None] * a[None, :]
        raff[:, j] += rd * rd

        rM = np.maximum(raff, unaff).max(axis=1)
        rSy = S - x + ref
        rr = rM / (rSy * rSy)
        bad2 = (~np.isfinite(rr)) | (rSy <= 0.0) | (rM < -1e-15)
        if np.any(bad2):
            rr[bad2] = np.inf

        j2 = int(np.argmin(rr))
        if rr[j2] < r_best:
            r_best = float(rr[j2])
            y_best = float(ref[j2])
            aff_best = raff[j2].copy()

    return y_best, aff_best, r_best


def coord_descent(a, time_limit, rng):
    """Block coordinate descent with stochastic restarts around best."""
    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        a = normalize_seq(a)
        return a, seq_M(a)

    a = normalize_seq(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 1:
        return a, 1.0

    b = full_conv(a)
    S = 1.0
    cur_M = float(np.max(b))
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_M = cur_M

    masks = build_unaff_momasks(n) if n <= 512 else None
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    time_limit = max(float(time_limit), 1.0)

    cycles = 0
    stall = 0
    noise = 0.01

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        order = rng.permutation(n)
        improved = False

        for j in order:
            if time.monotonic() - t0 > time_limit:
                break

            mask = masks[j] if masks is not None else None
            y, aff, r = coord_line_search(a, b, S, j, cur_M, mask)
            x = float(a[j])

            if np.isfinite(r) and y >= 0.0 and r < cur_M - 1e-14:
                b[j:j + n] = aff
                S = S - x + y
                a[j] = y
                cur_M = float(r)
                improved = True

                if cur_M < best_M - 1e-15:
                    best_M = cur_M
                    best_a = a.copy()

                if S > 3.0 or S < 0.3333333333333333:
                    a = a / S
                    b = b / (S * S)
                    S = 1.0
                    cur_M = float(np.max(b))
                    if cur_M < best_M - 1e-15:
                        best_M = cur_M
                        best_a = a.copy()

        cycles += 1

        if not improved:
            stall += 1
            cand = normalize_seq(best_a) + noise * rng.standard_normal(n)
            cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
            cand += 1e-12
            cand = cand / cand.sum()
            a = cand
            b = full_conv(a)
            S = 1.0
            cur_M = float(np.max(b))

            if cur_M < best_M - 1e-15:
                best_M = cur_M
                best_a = a.copy()

            if stall == 1:
                noise = 0.01
            else:
                noise = min(0.35, noise * 1.5)

            if stall >= 35 and noise > 0.30:
                break
        else:
            stall = 0
            if noise > 0.02:
                noise = max(0.01, noise * 0.8)

    return normalize_seq(best_a), float(best_M)


def lp_active_move(a, time_limit, rng):
    """Active-set linearized LP directions for max convolution."""
    if not HAS_LINPROG or time_limit <= 0.0:
        a = normalize_seq(a)
        return a, seq_M(a)

    a = normalize_seq(a)
    n = a.size
    b = full_conv(a)
    S = 1.0
    M = float(np.max(b))
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_M = M

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    fail = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        if M <= 0.0:
            break

        order = np.argsort(b)[::-1]
        Kbase = 10 + (it % 4) * 2
        K = min(b.size, Kbase)
        active = order[:K]

        if b.size > K + 2:
            hi = min(80, b.size)
            ex = int(rng.integers(K, hi))
            active = np.concatenate((active, order[ex], order[ex + 1]))
        active = np.unique(active)

        found = False
        deltas = (0.20, 0.10, 0.05, 0.02)

        for delta in deltas:
            A_ub = np.zeros((active.size, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
            b_ub = np.zeros(active.size, dtype=np.float64)

            for r, k in enumerate(active):
                k = int(k)
                js = k - np.arange(n)
                valid = (js >= 0) & (js < n)
                if np.any(valid):
                    A_ub[r, valid] = 2.0 * a[js[valid]]
                A_ub[r, -1] = -1.0
                b_ub[r] = -float(b[k])

            c_obj = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
            c_obj[-1] = 1.0

            low = -np.maximum(a, 0.0) * (1.0 + 1e-12)
            high = np.full(n, float(delta), dtype=np.float64)
            bounds = list(zip(low, high))
            bounds.append((0.0, float("inf")))

            A_eq = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
            A_eq[:n] = 1.0

            try:
                res = _linprog(
                    c_obj,
                    A_ub,
                    b_ub,
                    A_eq,
                    0.0,
                    bounds=bounds,
                    method="highs",
                )
            except Exception:
                res = None

            if (
                res is not None
                and getattr(res, "status", -1) in (0, 1)
                and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None
            ):
                d = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
                if np.all(np.isfinite(d)) and np.linalg.norm(d) > 1e-12:
                    best_alpha = None
                    best_newM = M
                    best_na = None
                    best_nb = None

                    for alpha in (1.0, 0.70, 0.50, 0.30, 0.20, 0.10, 0.05, 0.02):
                        na = a + alpha * d
                        mn = float(np.min(na))
                        if mn < -1e-7:
                            continue
                        na = np.maximum(na, 0.0)
                        ss = float(na.sum())
                        if ss <= 1e-300:
                            continue
                        na = na / ss
                        nb = full_conv(na)
                        newM = float(np.max(nb))
                        if newM < best_newM - 1e-14:
                            best_newM = newM
                            best_alpha = alpha
                            best_na = na.copy()
                            best_nb = nb.copy()

                    if best_alpha is not None and best_newM < M - 1e-14:
                        a = best_na
                        b = best_nb
                        M = best_newM
                        if M < best_M - 1e-14:
                            best_M = M
                            best_a = a.copy()
                        found = True
                        break

            it += 1

        if found:
            fail = 0
        else:
            fail += 1
            if fail >= 30:
                break

    return normalize_seq(best_a), float(best_M)


def build_dual_rows(n):
    """Rows for cross-convolution LP matrix."""
    rows = []
    m = 2 * n - 1
    for k in range(m):
        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(n - 1, k)
        if lo <= hi:
            rows.append((k, np.arange(lo, hi + 1, dtype=int)))
        else:
            rows.append((k, None))
    return rows


def dual_lp_move(a, time_limit, rng):
    """SOTA-style LP: maximize sum(g) subject to a*g <= M."""
    if not HAS_LINPROG or time_limit <= 0.0:
        a = normalize_seq(a)
        return a, seq_M(a)

    a = normalize_seq(a)
    n = a.size
    rows = build_dual_rows(n)
    b = full_conv(a)
    M = float(np.max(b))
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_M = M

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    fail = 0
    U = 4.0
    noise = 0.003

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        # Tiny positiveization for LP conditioning.
        a_lp = a + 1e-9
        a_lp = a_lp / a_lp.sum()
        b_lp = full_conv(a_lp)
        M_lp = float(np.max(b_lp))
        if M_lp <= 0.0:
            break

        found = False
        for lam in (1.0, 0.98, 0.95, 0.90):
            rhs = lam * M_lp
            if rhs <= 0.0:
                continue

            A = np.zeros((b.size, n), dtype=np.float64)
            for k, idx in rows:
                if idx is not None:
                    A[k, idx] = a_lp[k - idx]

            c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            bounds = [(0.0, float(U))] * n

            try:
                res = _linprog(
                    c_obj,
                    A,
                    np.full(b.size, float(rhs)),
                    bounds=bounds,
                    method="highs",
                )
            except Exception:
                res = None

            if (
                res is not None
                and getattr(res, "status", -1) in (0, 1)
                and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None
            ):
                g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
                gs = float(g.sum())
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)) or gs <= 1e-15:
                    continue
                g = g / gs

                best_alpha = None
                best_newM = M
                best_na = None
                best_nb = None

                for alpha in (0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.30, 0.50, 0.75, 1.00):
                    na = (1.0 - alpha) * a + alpha * g
                    na = np.maximum(na, 0.0)
                    ss = float(na.sum())
                    if ss <= 1e-300:
                        continue
                    na = na / ss
                    nb = full_conv(na)
                    newM = float(np.max(nb))
                    if newM < best_newM - 1e-14:
                        best_newM = newM
                        best_alpha = alpha
                        best_na = na.copy()
                        best_nb = nb.copy()

                if best_alpha is not None and best_newM < M - 1e-14:
                    a = best_na
                    b = best_nb
                    M = best_newM
                    if M < best_M - 1e-14:
                        best_M = M
                        best_a = a.copy()
                    found = True
                    break

        if found:
            fail = 0
            noise = max(0.001, noise * 0.7)
        else:
            fail += 1
            if fail >= 10:
                break
            a = normalize_seq(a + noise * rng.standard_normal(n))
            b = full_conv(a)
            M = float(np.max(b))
            noise = min(0.02, noise * 1.4)

    return normalize_seq(best_a), float(best_M)


def slsqp_obj(x, n):
    return x[-1]


def slsqp_obj_jac(x, n):
    v = np.zeros(x.size, dtype=np.float64)
    v[-1] = 1.0
    return v


def slsqp_eq(x, n):
    return np.array([np.sum(x[:n]) - 1.0], dtype=np.float64)


def slsqp_eq_jac(x, n):
    J = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    J[0, :n] = 1.0
    return J


def slsqp_ineq(x, active, n):
    a = x[:n]
    t = float(x[-1])
    out = np.empty(active.size, dtype=np.float64)
    for idx, k in enumerate(active):
        k = int(k)
        lo = max(0, k - (n - 1))
        hi = min(n - 1, k)
        if lo > hi:
            out[idx] = t
        else:
            i1 = np.arange(lo, hi + 1, dtype=int)
            i2 = k - i1
            out[idx] = t - float(np.dot(a[i1], a[i2]))
    return out


def slsqp_ineq_jac(x, active, n):
    a = x[:n]
    m = active.size
    J = np.zeros((m, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    for idx, k in enumerate(active):
        k = int(k)
        js = k - np.arange(n)
        valid = (js >= 0) & (js < n)
        if np.any(valid):
            J[idx, valid] = -2.0 * a[js[valid]]
        J[idx, -1] = 1.0
    return J


def slsqp_active(a, time_limit, rng):
    """Small active-set SLSQP polish; optional and time-capped."""
    if not HAS_SCIPY_OPT or time_limit <= 5.0:
        a = normalize_seq(a)
        return a, seq_M(a)

    a = normalize_seq(a)
    n = a.size
    b = full_conv(a)
    M = float(np.max(b))
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_M = M

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    outer = 0
    K = 8
    fail = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit and outer < 40:
        if M <= 0.0:
            break

        order = np.argsort(b)[::-1]
        active = order[:K]
        near = np.where(b > 0.98 * M)[0]
        active = np.unique(np.concatenate((active, near)))
        if active.size > 14:
            active = order[:14]

        x0 = np.concatenate((a, np.array([M * (1.0 + 1e-6)], dtype=np.float64)))
        bounds = [(0.0, float("inf"))] * n + [(0.0, 2.0)]

        eq = {"type": "eq", "fun": slsqp_eq, "jac": slsqp_eq_jac, "args": (n,)}
        ineq = {
            "type": "ineq",
            "fun": slsqp_ineq,
            "jac": slsqp_ineq_jac,
            "args": (active, n),
        }

        try:
            res = _so.minimize(
                slsqp_obj,
                x0,
                args=(n,),
                method="SLSQP",
                jac=slsqp_obj_jac,
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=[eq, ineq],
                options={"maxiter": 12, "ftol": 1e-9, "disp": False},
            )
        except Exception:
            res = None

        updated = False
        if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
            na = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
            na = np.where(np.isfinite(na), na, 0.0)
            na = np.maximum(na, 0.0)
            ss = float(na.sum())
            if ss > 1e-300:
                na = na / ss
                nb = full_conv(na)
                newM = float(np.max(nb))
                if newM < best_M - 1e-14:
                    best_M = newM
                    best_a = na.copy()
                    a = na.copy()
                    b = nb
                    M = newM
                    updated = True
                elif newM < M - 1e-14:
                    a = na.copy()
                    b = nb
                    M = newM

        if not updated:
            fail += 1
            if fail > 6:
                break
        else:
            fail = 0
            K = min(14, K + 2)

        outer += 1

    return normalize_seq(best_a), float(best_M)


def optimize_start(a, time_limit, rng, use_lp=True, use_slsqp=True):
    """Run a local optimization pipeline starting from a sequence."""
    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        a = normalize_seq(a)
        return a, seq_M(a)

    t_start = time.monotonic()
    a = normalize_seq(a)
    M = seq_M(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_M = M

    # Main coordinate pass.
    tb1 = max(1.0, 0.50 * float(time_limit))
    a1, M1 = coord_descent(a, tb1, rng)
    if M1 < best_M:
        best_M = M1
        best_a = a1.copy()

    rem = float(time_limit) - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    n_cur = best_a.size
    lp_allowed = bool(use_lp and HAS_LINPROG and n_cur <= 512)

    if lp_allowed and rem > 8.0:
        tb2 = max(1.0, min(rem * 0.30, float(time_limit) * 0.20, 180.0))
        a2, M2 = lp_active_move(best_a, tb2, rng)
        if M2 < best_M:
            best_M = M2
            best_a = a2.copy()
        rem = float(time_limit) - (time.monotonic() - t_start)

        if rem > 8.0:
            tb2b = max(1.0, min(rem * 0.30, float(time_limit) * 0.20, 180.0))
            a2b, M2b = dual_lp_move(best_a, tb2b, rng)
            if M2b < best_M:
                best_M = M2b
                best_a = a2b.copy()
            rem = float(time_limit) - (time.monotonic() - t_start)

    sl_allowed = bool(use_slsqp and HAS_SCIPY_OPT and n_cur <= 256)
    if sl_allowed and rem > 4.0:
        tb3 = max(4.0, min(rem * 0.40, 20.0))
        a3, M3 = slsqp_active(best_a, tb3, rng)
        if M3 < best_M:
            best_M = M3
            best_a = a3.copy()
        rem = float(time_limit) - (time.monotonic() - t_start)

    if rem - 1.0 > 2.0:
        tb4 = max(2.0, min(rem - 1.0, float(time_limit) * 0.25))
        a4, M4 = coord_descent(best_a, tb4, rng)
        if M4 < best_M:
            best_M = M4
            best_a = a4.copy()

    return normalize_seq(best_a), float(best_M)


def generate_inits(n, rng):
    """Diverse non-negative initial profiles."""
    inits = []
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    d = np.minimum(i, float(n - 1 - i))

    if n <= 512:
        # U-shaped profiles.
        for p in (-0.5, 0.0, 0.2, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.5):
            a = (d + 1.0) ** (-p) + 1e-8
            a = a + 0.002 * np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n))
            inits.append(normalize_seq(a))

        # Smooth edge-heavy exponentials.
        for lam in (0.1, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0):
            a = np.exp(-lam * (d / max(float(n), 1.0))) + 1e-8
            inits.append(normalize_seq(a))

        # One-sided power laws and mixed orientation.
        for p in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
            a = (i + 1.0) ** (-p) + 1e-8
            inits.append(normalize_seq(a))
            a = (n - i) ** (-p) + 1e-8
            inits.append(normalize_seq(a))

        # Cosine-like multi-bump profiles.
        if n > 2:
            denom = float(n - 1)
            for f in (1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 8):
                base = 2.0 * math.pi * float(f) * i / denom
                a = (1.0 + np.cos(base)) ** 2 + 1e-8
                inits.append(normalize_seq(a))
                a = (1.0 - np.cos(base)) ** 2 + 1e-8
                inits.append(normalize_seq(a))

        # Small Gaussian cluster mixtures.
        for _ in range(3):
            k = 2 + int(rng.integers(0, 2))
            centers = rng.uniform(0.0, float(n), size=k)
            a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            for c in centers:
                sigma = max(1.0, float(n) * (0.03 + 0.10 * float(rng.random())))
                a += np.exp(-((i - c) / sigma) ** 2)
            a += 0.01 * np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n))
            inits.append(normalize_seq(a))

        # Generic smooth and spike-like noise.
        for _ in range(2):
            a = 1.0 + 0.4 * rng.standard_normal(n)
            inits.append(normalize_seq(a))
        for _ in range(2):
            a = np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n)) + 0.01
            inits.append(normalize_seq(a))
    else:
        # For unexpectedly large n, keep construction cheap.
        inits.append(normalize_seq(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)))
        inits.append(normalize_seq((d + 1.0) ** (-0.5) + 1e-8))
        inits.append(normalize_seq((d + 1.0) ** (-1.0) + 1e-8))
        inits.append(normalize_seq(1.0 + 0.5 * rng.standard_normal(n)))

    if not inits:
        inits.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))

    return inits


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for the best coefficient sequence under the stated evaluator.
    Returns a Python list of non-negative floats.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(10.0, float(budget_s) - 30.0)

    prev_a = None
    n_target = 128

    try:
        h = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
        h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
        if h.size >= 2 and float(h.sum()) > 1e-9:
            prev_a = h
            n_target = int(h.size)
    except Exception:
        prev_a = None

    if n_target < 2:
        n_target = 128

    best_a = None
    best_M = float("inf")

    if prev_a is not None:
        a0 = normalize_seq(prev_a)
        m0 = seq_M(a0)
        if np.isfinite(m0):
            best_a = a0.copy()
            best_M = m0
        print(f"[init] previous construction n={n_target}, M={best_M:.9f}, score={2.0*n_target*best_M:.6f}", flush=True)

    inits = generate_inits(n_target, rng)
    inits_scored = []
    for idx, aa in enumerate(inits):
        mm = seq_M(aa)
        if np.isfinite(mm):
            inits_scored.append((float(mm), idx, aa))
    inits_scored.sort()

    if best_a is None and inits_scored:
        m0, _, a0 = inits_scored[0]
        best_a = a0.copy()
        best_M = float(m0)
        print(f"[init] best initial construction M={best_M:.9f}, score={2.0*n_target*best_M:.6f}", flush=True)

    total = max(60.0, deadline - time.monotonic())

    if prev_a is not None:
        primary_frac = 0.55
        explore_frac = 0.30
        final_frac = 0.15
    else:
        primary_frac = 0.35
        explore_frac = 0.45
        final_frac = 0.20

    # Primary optimization of the current best.
    if best_a is not None:
        primary_budget = total * primary_frac
        remain = deadline - time.monotonic() - 10.0
        tb = min(primary_budget, max(0.0, remain))
        if tb > 5.0:
            a_p, m_p = optimize_start(best_a, tb, rng, use_lp=True, use_slsqp=True)
            if m_p < best_M:
                best_M = m_p
                best_a = a_p.copy()
            print(f"[primary] M={best_M:.9f}, score={2.0*n_target*best_M:.6f}", flush=True)

    # Secondary exploration from diverse starts.
    if n_target <= 256:
        max_explore = 8
    elif n_target <= 1024:
        max_explore = 4
    else:
        max_explore = 2

    if prev_a is not None:
        pool = inits_scored[:max_explore]
    else:
        pool = inits_scored[1:1 + max_explore]

    if pool and best_a is not None:
        explore_budget = max(60.0, total * explore_frac)
        per = explore_budget / float(max(1, len(pool)))

        for item in pool:
            m_i, _, a_i = item
            remain = deadline - time.monotonic() - 5.0
            if remain < 15.0:
                break

            tb = per
            tb = min(tb, remain * 0.7, 240.0)

            if m_i > best_M * 3.0:
                tb *= 0.25
            elif m_i > best_M * 1.5:
                tb *= 0.5

            if tb > 8.0:
                use_lp_here = bool(tb > 40.0 and m_i < best_M * 1.3)
                a_e, m_e = optimize_start(a_i, tb, rng, use_lp=use_lp_here, use_slsqp=False)
                if m_e < best_M:
                    best_M = m_e
                    best_a = a_e.copy()
                print(f"[explore] start M_init={m_i:.7f}, best after start={m_e:.7f}, global={best_M:.7f}", flush=True)

    # Final polishing of best.
    if best_a is not None:
        remain = deadline - time.monotonic() - 5.0
        final_budget = max(30.0, total * final_frac)
        tb = min(final_budget, max(0.0, remain))
        if tb > 5.0:
            a_f, m_f = optimize_start(best_a, tb, rng, use_lp=True, use_slsqp=True)
            if m_f < best_M:
                best_M = m_f
                best_a = a_f.copy()
            print(f"[final] M={best_M:.9f}, score={2.0*n_target*best_M:.6f}", flush=True)

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(n_target, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_target)
        best_M = seq_M(best_a)

    result_arr = normalize_seq(best_a)
    result_arr = result_arr + 1e-12
    result_arr = result_arr / result_arr.sum()
    result_arr = np.clip(result_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    result = [float(x) for x in result_arr]

    try:
        final_eval = float(evaluate_sequence(result))
    except Exception:
        final_eval = float("inf")

    print(f"[done] n={len(result)}, internal_score={2.0*len(result)*best_M:.6f}, evaluator={final_eval:.6f}", flush=True)
    return result
```
